# Shader inventory

Generated by `python -I tools/phyre_shaders.py inventory` in the projectzanarkand repo from FFX_Data.vbf. Do not edit by hand. The prose is reversing/SHADERS.md.

## Totals

- .fx.phyre clusters: 793, distinct shader sources: 110
- how the game reaches them (sources / files): exe 34 / 34, import 8 / 691, manifest 63 / 63, none 5 / 5
- objects: PEffect 793, PEffectVariant 793, PSceneRenderPass 3566, PShader 10786, PShaderPass 8656, PShaderVertexProgram 3958, PShaderFragmentProgram 8649, PShaderGeometryProgram 0, PShaderComputeProgram 0, PShaderParameterDefinition 70056, PMaterial 793
- reached from the variants: 793 variants, 3566 scene render passes, 10779 shader references, 10811 shader pass references
- distinct DXBC blobs by MD5: 2515 (ps 2024, vs 491), 14109248 bytes, from 12607 program objects
- shader models: ps_4_0 2024, vs_4_0 491
- compilers (the RDEF creator string): 9.29.952.3111 2515
- blobs that are not DXBC containers: 0
- vertex input formats (DXGI, per distinct vertex blob element): 2 x 330, 6 x 811, 16 x 465, 30 x 180
- vertex input elements (semantic, PRenderDataType): BLENDINDICES0 SkinIndices x 180, BLENDWEIGHTS0 SkinWeights x 180, COLOR0 Color x 91, COLOR1 Color x 55, NORMAL0 Normal x 196, NORMAL0 SkinnableNormal x 114, POSITION0 SkinnableVertex x 180, POSITION0 Vertex x 295, TANGENT0 Tangent x 12, TEXCOORD0 ST x 426, TEXCOORD1 ST x 3, TEXCOORD4 ST x 10, TEXCOORD5 ST x 3, TEXCOORD6 ST x 13, TEXCOORD7 ST x 13, VERTEX0 Vertex x 11, WPOS0 ScreenVertex x 4
- model clusters importing an effect: 1266, imports that name no shipped effect: 0
- vfxShader_fx.txt names 63 sources, 63 of them shipped

## Sources

One row per shader source. Files is the number of .fx.phyre clusters (the plain one, or `#md5` material instances). Loader is how the game reaches the source: `exe` an FFX.exe path string, `manifest` the vfxShader_fx.txt list, `import` a PAssetReferenceImport in a .dae or .ags cluster, `none` nothing found. Importers is the number of model clusters that import any of the files, materials the PMaterial objects that point at them. Blobs is the distinct DXBC count over all the files of the source.

## Blobs

One row per distinct DXBC blob, keyed by the MD5 of the bytes (phyrecheck shaders --list prints the same key). Profile is the PShaderProgramD3D11 m_shaderProfile word, model, instr and the declaration counts come from D3DDisassemble of the blob: instructions, constant buffers (register[size in vec4]), samplers, textures, input and output declarations. Input layout is the PStreamInputDescD3D11 list of a vertex program, semantic and index : DXGI format / slot. Entry points are the PShaderPassInfo names that select the blob, sources the shader sources whose clusters contain it.
